Every command validates all input and rules before its first write, and a rejected command\nchanges nothing.\n\n## Technology Stack\n\n- Python 3.11+ — implementation language.\n- SQLite — database, reached through the shipped persistence library (`persistence.db`,\n  `open_database(dsn)`).\n- CLI — the `ledger` executable at the application root, calling `cli.main`.\n- pytest — test runner behind `bin/test.sh`.\n- POSIX sh — `bin/test.sh` and the supplied grader.\n\n## Foundation Layout\n\n- `pyproject.toml` declares Python 3.11 or newer and `pytest`. `uv.lock` is committed.\n- `.venv` is created by `uv venv`.\n- `bin/test.sh` is a POSIX wrapper that runs `pytest -q` from the application root.\n- `ledger` at the application root calls `cli.main`.\n\n## Modules\n\n| Module | Responsibility |\n|--------|----------------|\n| `cli` | Command-line entry point, dispatch table, usage errors, help text, CSV output. |\n| `persistence` | Generated and shipped database package. Application code reaches the store only through it. |\n| `rules` | Amount codec, code/name/memo validation, date validation, non-negative balance check. |\n| `load` | Shared CSV load path: header check, row validation, all-or-nothing write. |\n| `commands` | Account, transaction, transfer, schema, and report command handlers. |\n\n## Boundaries\n\n| Boundary | Owner | Allowed access |\n|----------|-------|----------------|\n| Database location | `cli` (resolves `DATABASE_URL`, then `DATABASE_PATH`, then `data/app.db`) | Passes the DSN to `persistence.open_database`. |\n| Store access | `persistence` | Only `persistence.db.<table>.<verb>(...)` callers. No driver import outside the library. |\n| Business rules | `rules` | Pure functions; no storage access. |\n| Command handlers | `commands` | Call `rules` and `persistence`; never a driver. |\n\n## Module Ownership\n\n| Boundary | Module that owns it | Low-level APIs that module may access |\n|----------|---------------------|----------------------------------------|\n| Persistence | `persistence` (generated from `DATABASE.md`) | `sqlite3` via the shipped library only |\n| Configuration | `cli` | `os.environ` for the three location variables |\n| File stores | None | Not used |\n| External services | None | Not used |\n\n## Technical Decisions\n\n- Amounts are stored and computed as integer cents. No floating point at any layer.\n- Each command checks every rule before its first write, so a rejected command writes nothing.\n- The database DSN selects the engine: `postgres://` and `postgresql://` are PostgreSQL DSNs; any\n  other value is a SQLite file path, optionally prefixed `sqlite://`.\n- Exit codes follow the source contract: 0 success, 1 operational, 2 usage, 3 rejected, 4 not found.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n- None. Every command validates all input and rules before its first write, and a rejected command\nchanges nothing.\n\n## Technology Stack\n\n- Python 3.11+ — implementation language.\n- SQLite — database, reached through the shipped persistence library (`persistence.db`,\n  `open_database(dsn)`).\n- CLI — the `ledger` executable at the application root, calling `cli.main`.\n- pytest — test runner behind `bin/test.sh`.\n- POSIX sh — `bin/test.sh` and the supplied grader.\n\n## Foundation Layout\n\n- `pyproject.toml` declares Python 3.11 or newer and `pytest`. `uv.lock` is committed.\n- `.venv` is created by `uv venv`.\n- `bin/test.sh` is a POSIX wrapper that runs `pytest -q` from the application root.\n- `ledger` at the application root calls `cli.main`.\n\n## Modules\n\n| Module | Responsibility |\n|--------|----------------|\n| `cli` | Command-line entry point, dispatch table, usage errors, help text, CSV output. |\n| `persistence` | Generated and shipped database package. Application code reaches the store only through it. |\n| `rules` | Amount codec, code/name/memo validation, date validation, non-negative balance check. |\n| `load` | Shared CSV load path: header check, row validation, all-or-nothing write. |\n| `commands` | Account, transaction, transfer, schema, and report command handlers. |\n\n## Boundaries\n\n| Boundary | Owner | Allowed access |\n|----------|-------|----------------|\n| Database location | `cli` (resolves `DATABASE_URL`, then `DATABASE_PATH`, then `data/app.db`) | Passes the DSN to `persistence.open_database`. |\n| Store access | `persistence` | Only `persistence.db.<table>.<verb>(...)` callers. No driver import outside the library. |\n| Business rules | `rules` | Pure functions; no storage access. |\n| Command handlers | `commands` | Call `rules` and `persistence`; never a driver. |\n\n## Module Ownership\n\n| Boundary | Module that owns it | Low-level APIs that module may access |\n|----------|---------------------|----------------------------------------|\n| Persistence | `persistence` (generated from `DATABASE.md`) | `sqlite3` via the shipped library only |\n| Configuration | `cli` | `os.environ` for the three location variables |\n| File stores | None | Not used |\n| External services | None | Not used |\n\n## Technical Decisions\n\n- Amounts are stored and computed as integer cents. No floating point at any layer.\n- Each command checks every rule before its first write, so a rejected command writes nothing.\n- The database DSN selects the engine: `postgres://` and `postgresql://` are PostgreSQL DSNs; any\n  other value is a SQLite file path, optionally prefixed `sqlite://`.\n- Exit codes follow the source contract: 0 success, 1 operational, 2 usage, 3 rejected, 4 not found.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n- None. Architecture is verified through the feature and story acceptance that implements it.\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Do not open a database driver outside the shipped persistence library.\n- Do not hardcode report rows, counts, or totals.\n- Do not read `sources/run.sh` or any seed file from application code.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-Database-Location.md ===\n# FEATURE: Database Location\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261021 V1 |\n| Description | Resolve the database location and open the store through the persistence library. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md |\n| Provides    | open_database configuration |\n| Consumes    | |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nSelect the store named by `DATABASE_URL`, then `DATABASE_PATH`, then `data/app.db`, and open it\nthrough `persistence.open_database`. A `postgres://` or `postgresql://` value is a PostgreSQL DSN\nand is never treated as a file path.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by FEATURE-Schema-Init.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC database-location-precedence ===\nIntent: DATABASE_URL takes precedence over DATABASE_PATH when both are set.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nimport os\nfrom cli.config import database_dsn\n\nos.environ[\"DATABASE_URL\"] = \"/tmp/url-wins.db\"\nos.environ[\"DATABASE_PATH\"] = \"/tmp/path-loses.db\"\ndsn = database_dsn()\nassert dsn == \"/tmp/url-wins.db\"\n=== END AC database-location-precedence ===\n\n=== AC database-location-default ===\nIntent: With neither variable set, the default store is data/app.db.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nimport os\nfrom cli.config import database_dsn\n\nos.environ.pop(\"DATABASE_URL\", None)\nos.environ.pop(\"DATABASE_PATH\", None)\ndsn = database_dsn()\nassert dsn == \"data/app.db\"\n=== END AC database-location-default ===\n\n=== AC database-location-postgres ===\nIntent: A postgres:// DSN is classified as PostgreSQL and never as a file path.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nimport os\nfrom cli.config import database_dsn, is_postgres_dsn\n\nos.environ[\"DATABASE_URL\"] = \"postgres://user@localhost/ledger\"\ndsn = database_dsn()\nassert dsn == \"postgres://user@localhost/ledger\"\nassert is_postgres_dsn(dsn) is True\n=== END AC database-location-postgres ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Never treat a `postgres://` or `postgresql://` DSN as a file path.\n- Never open a database driver directly; reach the store only through `persistence.open_database`.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-CLI-Entry.md ===\n# FEATURE: CLI Entry\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261021 V1 |\n| Description | The `ledger` executable at the application root and its command dispatch. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md, FEATURE-Database-Location.md |\n| Provides    | ./ledger, dispatch registry |\n| Consumes    | |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nProvide `ledger` at the application root. It calls `cli.main` and dispatches each command through\none table. scope=test\n\nfrom cli import main\nfrom cli.dispatch import COMMANDS\n\nassert \"count\" in COMMANDS\nassert callable(COMMANDS[\"count\"])\n=== END AC cli-entry-dispatch ===\n\n=== AC cli-entry-unknown-command ===\nIntent: An unknown command returns the usage exit code 2 with no standard output.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\n\nresult = subprocess.run(\n    [sys.executable, \"ledger\", \"init\"],\n    capture_output=True, text=True,\n    env={**__import__(\"os\").environ, \"DATABASE_URL\": \"/tmp/schema-init.db\"},\n)\nprint(result.stderr, file=sys.stderr)\nassert result.returncode == 0\nassert result.stdout == \"\"\n=== END AC schema-init-empty ===\n\n=== AC schema-init-recreates ===\nIntent: Init called twice leaves zero rows, proving drop-and-recreate.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nimport os\n\nenv = {**os.environ, \"DATABASE_URL\": \"/tmp/schema-init-twice.db\"}\nfirst = subprocess.run([sys.executable, \"ledger\", \"init\"], capture_output=True, text=True, env=env)\nsecond = subprocess.run([sys.executable, \"ledger\", \"init\"], capture_output=True, text=True, env=env)\nprint(first.stderr, second.stderr, file=sys.stderr)\nassert first.returncode == 0\nassert second.returncode == 0\nassert second.stdout == \"\"\n=== END AC schema-init-recreates ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- `init` writes nothing to standard output.\n- Init drops and recreates all three tables; it never migrates existing tables.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-Schema-Count.md ===\n# FEATURE: Schema Count\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261021 V1 |\n| Description | `ledger count` prints row counts per table in fixed order. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md, FEATURE-Schema-Init.md |\n| Provides    | ledger count |\n| Consumes    | |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger count` prints `table,rows` followed by the `accounts`, `categories`, and `transactions`\nrow counts, read from the database, in that order.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-SCHEMA-002` — read: count rows in each declared table - returns: `rows`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC schema-count-header ===\nIntent: The count report begins with the table,rows header line.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nimport os\n\nenv = {**os.environ, \"DATABASE_URL\": \"/tmp/schema-count.db\"}\nsubprocess.run([sys.executable, \"ledger\", \"init\"], capture_output=True, text=True, env=env)\nresult = subprocess.run([sys.executable, \"ledger\", \"count\"], capture_output=True, text=True, env=env)\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr, file=sys.stderr)\nassert result.returncode == 0\nlines = result.stdout.splitlines()\nassert lines[0] == \"table,rows\"\n=== END AC schema-count-header ===\n\n=== AC schema-count-order ===\nIntent: The count rows appear in accounts, categories, transactions order.\nRequires: executable=python3;
